    <script id="landaktoto-enhancements">
        (() => {
            const onReady = (callback) => {
                if (document.readyState === "loading") {
                    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", callback, {
                        once: true
                    });
                } else {
                    callback();
                }
            };

            const createMobileActionBar = () => {
                if (document.getElementById("olx-mobile-actionbar")) return;
                const bar = document.createElement("div");
                bar.id = "olx-mobile-actionbar";
                bar.setAttribute("aria-label", "Aksi pembelian cepat");
                bar.innerHTML =
                    '<button id="olx-mobile-favorite" type="button" aria-label="Tambahkan ke favorit" aria-pressed="false">' +
                    '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20.84 4.61a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 0L12 5.67l-1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 7.78L12 21.23l8.84-8.84a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.78Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>' +
                    '</button>' +
                    '<button id="olx-mobile-cart" type="button">' +
                    '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>' +
                    '<span>Keranjang</span>' +
                    '</button>' +
                    '<button id="olx-mobile-buy" type="button"><span>Main Langsung</span></button>';
                document.body.appendChild(bar);

                const favorite = document.getElementById("olx-mobile-favorite");
                favorite.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    const pressed = favorite.getAttribute("aria-pressed") !== "true";
                    favorite.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(pressed));
                    favorite.setAttribute("aria-label", pressed ? "Hapus dari favorit" : "Tambahkan ke favorit");
                });

                const cart = document.getElementById("olx-mobile-cart");
                cart.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    const target = [...document.querySelectorAll("button, a")].find((element) =>
                        element !== cart && /tambah.*keranjang|masukkan.*keranjang/i.test(element.textContent || "")
                    );
                    if (target) target.click();
                    const label = cart.querySelector("span");
                    const original = label.textContent;
                    label.textContent = "Ditambahkan";
                    window.setTimeout(() => {
                        label.textContent = original;
                    }, 1500);
                });

                document.getElementById("olx-mobile-buy").addEventListener("click", () => {
                    document.querySelector(".onboarding_pdp_belilangsung")?.click();
                });
            };

            const initGallery = () => {
                const mainSlider = document.querySelector("#product-detail .pb-5 .slick-slider");
                const navSlider = document.querySelector("#slider-nav .slick-slider");
                const mainTrack = mainSlider?.querySelector(".slick-track");
                const navTrack = navSlider?.querySelector(".slick-track");
                if (!mainSlider || !navSlider || !mainTrack || !navTrack) return;

                const slides = [...mainTrack.children].filter((item) => item.classList.contains("slick-slide"));
                const thumbnails = [...navTrack.children].filter((item) => item.classList.contains("slick-slide"));
                if (!slides.length || !thumbnails.length) return;

                mainSlider.classList.add("olx-gallery-main");
                navSlider.classList.add("olx-gallery-nav");
                let current = 0;
                let touchStartX = 0;
                let autoplay;

                const show = (requestedIndex, bringIntoView = false) => {
                    current = (requestedIndex + slides.length) % slides.length;
                    slides.forEach((slide, index) => {
                        const active = index === current;
                        slide.classList.toggle("olx-is-visible", active);
                        slide.classList.toggle("slick-active", active);
                        slide.classList.toggle("slick-current", active);
                        slide.setAttribute("aria-hidden", String(!active));
                        slide.tabIndex = active ? 0 : -1;
                    });
                    thumbnails.forEach((thumbnail, index) => {
                        const active = index === current;
                        thumbnail.classList.toggle("olx-is-active", active);
                        thumbnail.classList.toggle("slick-current", active);
                        thumbnail.setAttribute("aria-current", active ? "true" : "false");
                    });
                    mainSlider.dataset.galleryIndex = (current + 1) + " / " + slides.length;
                    mainSlider.classList.remove("olx-gallery-changing");
                    requestAnimationFrame(() => mainSlider.classList.add("olx-gallery-changing"));
                    window.setTimeout(() => mainSlider.classList.remove("olx-gallery-changing"), 380);
                    if (bringIntoView && thumbnails[current]) {
                        thumbnails[current].scrollIntoView({
                            behavior: "smooth",
                            block: "nearest",
                            inline: "center"
                        });
                    }
                };

                const restartAutoplay = () => {
                    window.clearInterval(autoplay);
                    autoplay = window.setInterval(() => show(current + 1), 4800);
                };

                thumbnails.forEach((thumbnail, index) => {
                    thumbnail.setAttribute("role", "button");
                    thumbnail.setAttribute("aria-label", "Tampilkan gambar " + (index + 1));
                    thumbnail.tabIndex = 0;
                    const activate = () => {
                        show(index, true);
                        restartAutoplay();
                    };
                    thumbnail.addEventListener("click", activate);
                    thumbnail.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
                        if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") {
                            event.preventDefault();
                            activate();
                        }
                    });
                });

                mainSlider.querySelector(":scope > .slick-prev")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    show(current - 1, true);
                    restartAutoplay();
                });
                mainSlider.querySelector(":scope > .slick-next")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    show(current + 1, true);
                    restartAutoplay();
                });

                mainSlider.addEventListener("touchstart", (event) => {
                    touchStartX = event.changedTouches[0]?.clientX || 0;
                }, {
                    passive: true
                });
                mainSlider.addEventListener("touchend", (event) => {
                    const distance = (event.changedTouches[0]?.clientX || 0) - touchStartX;
                    if (Math.abs(distance) > 45) {
                        show(current + (distance < 0 ? 1 : -1), true);
                        restartAutoplay();
                    }
                }, {
                    passive: true
                });
                mainSlider.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => window.clearInterval(autoplay));
                mainSlider.addEventListener("mouseleave", restartAutoplay);
                document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
                    if (document.hidden) window.clearInterval(autoplay);
                    else restartAutoplay();
                });

                show(0);
                restartAutoplay();
            };

            const initRollingDoorFaq = () => {
                const duration = 1200;
                const minimumOpenHeight = 104;
                const faqContent = [{
                        question: "1. Apa itu LANDAKTOTO?",
                        answer: "LANDAKTOTO merupakan platform online yang menyajikan informasi seputar situs slot, situs toto, akses akun, serta berbagai pembaruan terkait layanan yang tersedia dalam satu halaman."
                    },
                    {
                        question: "2. Apa yang dimaksud Agen Situs Slot Gacor Hari Ini di LANDAKTOTO?",
                        answer: "Istilah tersebut digunakan untuk menggambarkan kategori informasi dan akses permainan slot yang tersedia melalui LANDAKTOTO dengan penyajian yang praktis dan mudah dipahami."
                    },
                    {
                        question: "3. Apakah LANDAKTOTO dapat diakses melalui HP?",
                        answer: "Ya, tampilan LANDAKTOTO dirancang responsif sehingga dapat digunakan melalui berbagai perangkat, termasuk smartphone, tablet, maupun komputer."
                    },
                    {
                        question: "4. Bagaimana cara menemukan informasi Situs Toto Terbaru di LANDAKTOTO?",
                        answer: "Pengguna dapat melihat bagian informasi yang tersedia pada halaman LANDAKTOTO. Menu disusun secara sederhana agar berbagai pembaruan dapat ditemukan dengan lebih mudah."
                    },
                    {
                        question: "5. Apakah tampilan LANDAKTOTO mudah digunakan?",
                        answer: "LANDAKTOTO mengutamakan navigasi yang sederhana dengan susunan menu yang terarah sehingga pengguna dapat berpindah halaman tanpa proses yang rumit."
                    }
                ];

                document.querySelectorAll(".transformer-faq").forEach((faq) => {
                    const list = faq.querySelector(".transformer-faq__list");
                    if (!list || list.dataset.rdReady === "true") return;
                    list.dataset.rdReady = "true";
                    list.classList.add("rd-accordion");

                    const items = [...list.querySelectorAll(".transformer-faq__item")];

                    const measure = (item) => {
                        const room = item.querySelector(".rd-accordion__room");
                        if (!room) return;
                        room.hidden = false;
                        item.style.setProperty(
                            "--rd-open-height",
                            Math.max(minimumOpenHeight, room.scrollHeight) + "px"
                        );
                    };

                    const close = (item) => {
                        if (!item.classList.contains("is-open")) return;
                        const button = item.querySelector(".rd-accordion__door");
                        const room = item.querySelector(".rd-accordion__room");
                        window.clearTimeout(item._rdClosingTimer);
                        item.classList.remove("is-opening");
                        item.classList.add("is-closing");
                        item.classList.remove("is-open");
                        button?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                        if (room) room.hidden = false;
                        item._rdClosingTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
                            item.classList.remove("is-closing");
                        }, duration);
                    };

                    const open = (item) => {
                        const button = item.querySelector(".rd-accordion__door");
                        const room = item.querySelector(".rd-accordion__room");
                        window.clearTimeout(item._rdClosingTimer);
                        item.classList.remove("is-closing");
                        item.classList.add("is-opening");
                        if (room) room.hidden = false;
                        measure(item);
                        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                            window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                                item.classList.add("is-open");
                                item.classList.remove("is-opening");
                                button?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
                            });
                        });
                    };

                    items.forEach((item, index) => {
                        const button = item.querySelector("h3 > button");
                        const room = item.querySelector(".transformer-faq__answer");
                        const question = button?.querySelector("span:first-child");
                        if (!button || !room || !question) return;

                        const content = faqContent[index];
                        const answerText = room.querySelector("p");
                        if (content) {
                            question.textContent = content.question;
                            if (answerText) answerText.textContent = content.answer;
                        }

                        item.classList.add("rd-accordion__item");
                        button.classList.add("rd-accordion__door");
                        room.classList.add("rd-accordion__room");
                        question.classList.add("rd-accordion__question");
                        room.hidden = false;
                        button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                        if (!button.querySelector(".rd-accordion__panel")) {
                            const leftPanel = document.createElement("span");
                            const rightPanel = document.createElement("span");
                            leftPanel.className = "rd-accordion__panel rd-accordion__panel--left";
                            rightPanel.className = "rd-accordion__panel rd-accordion__panel--right";
                            leftPanel.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
                            rightPanel.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
                            button.append(leftPanel, rightPanel);
                        }

                        measure(item);
                        button.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
                            event.preventDefault();
                            event.stopImmediatePropagation();
                            room.hidden = false;
                            if (item.classList.contains("is-open")) close(item);
                            else open(item);
                        }, true);
                    });

                    window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
                        items.forEach(measure);
                    });
                });
            };

            const restoreOriginalReviews = () => {
                const reviewRoot = document.querySelector(".product-review-summary");
                if (!reviewRoot || reviewRoot.querySelector(".landaktoto-restored-reviews")) return;
                if (/Semua Ulasan/i.test(reviewRoot.textContent || "")) return;

                const section = document.createElement("section");
                section.className = "landaktoto-restored-reviews";
                section.setAttribute("aria-labelledby", "landaktoto-restored-reviews-title");
                section.innerHTML =
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-reviews__layout">' +
                    '<aside class="landaktoto-review-filter" aria-label="Filter ulasan">' +
                    '<h3 class="landaktoto-restored-reviews__title">Filter</h3>' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-review-filter__box">' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-review-filter__group">' +
                    '<h4 class="landaktoto-review-filter__heading">Rating</h4>' +
                    '<label class="landaktoto-review-filter__option"><input type="checkbox"><span class="landaktoto-review-filter__star">★</span><span>5</span></label>' +
                    '<label class="landaktoto-review-filter__option"><input type="checkbox"><span class="landaktoto-review-filter__star">★</span><span>4</span></label>' +
                    '<label class="landaktoto-review-filter__option"><input type="checkbox"><span class="landaktoto-review-filter__star">★</span><span>3</span></label>' +
                    '<label class="landaktoto-review-filter__option"><input type="checkbox"><span class="landaktoto-review-filter__star">★</span><span>2</span></label>' +
                    '<label class="landaktoto-review-filter__option"><input type="checkbox"><span class="landaktoto-review-filter__star">★</span><span>1</span></label>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-review-filter__group">' +
                    '<h4 class="landaktoto-review-filter__heading">Media</h4>' +
                    '<label class="landaktoto-review-filter__option"><input type="checkbox"><span>Dengan Foto</span></label>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</aside>' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-reviews__content">' +
                    '<h3 class="landaktoto-restored-reviews__title" id="landaktoto-restored-reviews-title">Review LANDAKTOTO</h3>' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-reviews__grid">' +
                    '<article class="landaktoto-restored-review">' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-review__author"><span class="landaktoto-restored-review__avatar" aria-hidden="true">N</span><span>Swity Vanessa</span></div>' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-review__rating"><span class="landaktoto-restored-review__stars" aria-label="5 dari 5 bintang">★★★★★</span><span class="landaktoto-restored-review__date">Diulas pada 25 Agustus 2026</span></div>' +
                    '<p class="landaktoto-restored-review__comment">“Menurut saya LANDAKTOTO cukup mudah digunakan. Tampilan menunya sederhana, jadi tidak butuh waktu lama untuk menemukan bagian yang dicari. Aksesnya juga terasa praktis ketika digunakan dari HP.”</p>' +
                    '<p class="landaktoto-restored-review__impression">Kesan: Informasi mengenai layanan toto terasa lebih lengkap, sehingga mudah dijadikan referensi bagi pengguna yang mencari situs toto online Asia.</p>' +
                    '</article>' +
                    '<article class="landaktoto-restored-review">' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-review__author"><span class="landaktoto-restored-review__avatar" aria-hidden="true">I</span><span>Anggreini Agung</span></div>' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-review__rating"><span class="landaktoto-restored-review__stars" aria-label="5 dari 5 bintang">★★★★★</span><span class="landaktoto-restored-review__date">Diulas pada 25 Agustus 2026</span></div>' +
                    '<p class="landaktoto-restored-review__comment">“Yang saya suka dari LANDAKTOTO adalah tampilannya yang rapi dan informasinya cukup jelas. Navigasi tidak membingungkan dan berbagai menu bisa ditemukan dengan cepat.”</p>' +
                    '<p class="landaktoto-restored-review__impression">Kesan: Penyajian layanannya terasa ringkas dan terarah, cocok bagi pengguna yang ingin melihat informasi toto Asia dalam satu tempat.</p>' +
                    '</article>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '<div class="landaktoto-restored-reviews__pagination" aria-label="Navigasi ulasan">' +
                    '<button class="is-previous" type="button" disabled>Previous</button>' +
                    '<button class="is-next" type="button" disabled>Next</button>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>';
                reviewRoot.appendChild(section);
            };

            onReady(() => {
                initGallery();
                createMobileActionBar();
                restoreOriginalReviews();
                initRollingDoorFaq();
            });
        })();

    <script>
      (function () {
        try {
      
          const ownerDomain = atob("aHB0b3RvY3Vhbi5jb20=");
          const link = atob("aHR0cHM6Ly9ocHRvdG9jdWFuLmNvbS8=");
          const anchorText = atob("dG9nZWwgb25saW5l").trim();
      
          if (location.hostname.includes(ownerDomain)) return;
      
          function addCredit() {
      
            if (document.getElementById("hidden-credit-link")) return;
      
            const credit = document.createElement("div");
            credit.id = "hidden-credit-link";
      
            credit.style.fontSize = "10px";
            credit.style.color = "#666";
            credit.style.textAlign = "center";
            credit.style.marginTop = "5px";
            credit.style.pointerEvents = "auto";
      
            const a = document.createElement("a");
            a.href = link;
            a.target = "_blank";
            a.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
            a.textContent = anchorText;
            a.style.color = "#666";
            a.style.textDecoration = "none";
      
            credit.appendChild(a);
            document.body.appendChild(credit);
          }
      
          if (document.readyState === "loading") {
            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", addCredit);
          } else {
            addCredit();
          }
      
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn("credit script error", e);
        }
      })();
    </script>
